### [1] [XENONnT $pp$-Dominated Solar Neutrinos Constrain the $ν_1$ Pseudo-Dirac Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38233)
*Gio Leone*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: XENONnT实验通过低能太阳中微子（主要是pp中微子）信号，对第一质量本征态的pseudo-Dirac振荡进行限制，排除了δm₁² ≳ 5.9×10⁻¹³ eV²（2σ）的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用太阳-地球基线上的长距离传播与XENONnT对低能电子反冲谱的高灵敏度，探测超小pseudo-Dirac质量劈裂δm₁²所诱导的主动-惰性中微子振荡效应。

Method: 建模第一质量本征态的pseudo-Dirac振荡，计算其对pp+⁷Be中微子引起的电子反冲能谱的修正，并基于XENONnT公布的能谱数据构造似然函数进行参数拟合与统计推断。

Result: 最佳拟合值为δm₁² = 0；在2σ置信水平下，排除δm₁² > 5.9×10⁻¹³ eV²；该灵敏度已逼近此前理论预言的未来pp敏感探测器可探区间。

Conclusion: XENONnT首次在直接探测实验中对极小尺度的pseudo-Dirac质量劈裂给出强约束，表明惰性中微子若以pseudo-Dirac形式存在，其质量简并性必须极高（δm₁² < 5.9×10⁻¹³ eV²）。

Abstract: XENONnT has measured a low-energy solar-neutrino signal dominated by $pp$ neutrinos. At these energies, an ultra-small pseudo-Dirac mass-squared splitting induces detectable active-sterile oscillations over the Sun--Earth baseline. We study pseudo-Dirac oscillations of the first mass eigenstate, compute the resulting modification of the $pp+{}^7$Be electron-recoil spectrum, and construct a likelihood from the published XENONnT energy spectra. We find that the best fit occurs at zero pseudo-Dirac splitting and rule out values of $δm_1^2 \gtrsim 5.9\times10^{-13}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$ at $2 σ$, approaching a regime previously discussed for future $pp$-sensitive detectors.

</details>


### [2] [Searching for BSM Experimental Signatures with Large Lagrangian Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38309)
*Ibrahim Elsharkawy,Victoria Knapp-Perez,Wahid Bhimji,Aishik Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出hAIthem框架，结合强化学习（RL）与大语言模型（LLM），在高维理论参数空间中自主搜索未被现有实验排除的BSM模型区域，并生成可检验的物理可观测量；在单暗标量多重态模型中验证其优于进化算法基线，发现新颖可观测量组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSM物理探索受限于缺乏判别性实验观测，尤其暗物质模型空间庞大而引力证据不足以区分；需系统性探索可检验的理论签名以指导未来实验设计。

Method: 构建基于自回归Transformer的Large Lagrangian Model（LLaM），预训练于约10,000个拉格朗日量（~1B tokens），并在实时环境中微调为RL agent；该agent通过Battleship式博弈对抗现象学工具套件，定位未被约束排除的参数子区域；随后构造基于可观测量的决策树，并由竞争性LLM agents生成物理上自洽的签名。

Result: 在单暗标量多重态受限模型空间中，hAIthem的RL搜索优于进化算法基线，发现更多、物理多样性更高的可行区域；并提出新颖可观测量组合，例如将halo-independent运动学比应用于古探测器（paleo-detectors）。

Conclusion: hAIthem实现了理论驱动与数据驱动方法的协同：RL实现高效参数空间导航，LLM注入文献知识并生成可解释签名；为BSM模型筛选与可观测量发现提供了可扩展、自动化的新范式。

Abstract: The search for physics Beyond the Standard Model (BSM) is generally limited not by the supply of theory descriptions but by the lack of discriminating experimental observations. A case in point is dark matter, where the overwhelming gravitational evidence only goes so far in distinguishing between models within a vast theory space. Exploring the space of testable model signatures may help identify overlooked experimental observables and indicate the utility of future experiments. A challenge is designing a search through model signatures outside what is found in the literature. Our primary contribution is hAIthem, a framework that combines the self-guided exploration of reinforcement learning (RL) with the broad literature-derived knowledge of LLMs. We build an RL agent that learns to find which portions of a theory's high-dimensional parameter space are not excluded under some subset of constraints by playing a Battleship-style "game" against a suite of phenomenology tools. The agent is built as a Large Lagrangian Model (LLaM), an autoregressive transformer that reads a tokenized Lagrangian, is pretrained at scale (here on ~1 billion tokens from ~10,000 Lagrangians), and is fine-tuned in a live environment. The framework then constructs a decision tree that separates RL-found regions using observables computed with established tools, and passes the remaining degenerate regions to a set of LLM agents that compete to produce realistic signatures. In this proof of concept, RL-search outperforms an evolutionary-algorithm baseline, finding more viable regions with greater physical diversity. In a restricted space of single dark scalar multiplet models, we find that hAIthem proposes interesting combinations of previously studied observables, such as the application of a halo-independent kinematic ratio to paleo-detectors.

</details>


### [3] [How collective modes terminate in the dispersion relation of neutrino plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38314)
*Damiano F. G. Fiorillo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非零中微子质量对中微子等离子体中味波色散关系奇异性的影响，区分并分类了两类奇异性：简并点（两模式合并）和模式突截（频率趋近于介电函数分支切口），并指出非零质量会消除轴对称快极限下的简并点，但模式突截在一般能量分布下仍普遍存在；此外，证明了洛伦兹型频率分布的无碰撞等离子体等价于具有有效碰撞率的单色系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非零中微子质量如何修正味波色散关系中的奇异性，特别是Landau阻尼模式在c→v_phase极限下的消失行为，这对超新星和早期宇宙中微子集体振荡建模至关重要。

Method: 基于线性色散理论，分析味介电函数ε(k,ω)的复平面上分支结构；对频率ω(k)展开局域级数，识别其作为波矢k函数的奇异性类型（代数简并 vs. 分支切口导致的非解析终止）；利用速度分布展宽与质量关联，比较零质量和非零质量情形下的对称性破缺。

Result: 1) 非零质量消除轴对称快极限中沿对称轴的简并点，可能仅存离轴简并线；2) 模式突截是通用现象，仅当能量分布为整函数时例外；3) 洛伦兹型频率分布严格等价于带有效衰减率Γ_eff的单色系统；4) 任意分布可由洛伦兹叠加高效逼近，大幅简化色散方程求解。

Conclusion: 中微子质量通过打破速度简并和展宽相速度阈值，从根本上改变味波的奇异性结构；模式突截比简并更鲁棒，是集体不稳定性分析的关键特征；洛伦兹展开法为实际数值计算提供了严格而高效的近似框架。

Abstract: Neutrino plasmas support flavor waves, collective oscillations of flavor, that neutrinos can resonantly emit or absorb when they fulfill the Cherenkov condition. For massless neutrinos, this condition leads to singular features in the dispersion relation: Landau-damped modes, for example, disappear abruptly as their phase velocity approaches the speed of light. We investigate how these singularities change when nonzero neutrino masses introduce a spread in velocities. We classify singular behavior of the frequency as a function of wavevector into i) degeneracy points, where two modes merge, and ii) abrupt mode termination, when the frequency approaches a branch cut of the flavor dielectric function. For each class, we derive a local expansion and determine where such behavior can occur. Degeneracy points, frequently encountered in axially symmetric dispersion relations in the fast limit, are not generic. Nonzero neutrino masses lift the degeneracy along the symmetry axis; in three-dimensional wavevector space, there might still be degeneracy lines, not necessarily intersecting the symmetry axis. Abrupt mode termination, instead, persists generically, except for special energy distributions which are entire functions. As a byproduct of this study, we show that a collisionless plasma with a Lorentzian neutrino-frequency distribution is exactly equivalent to a monochromatic one with an effective collision rate. Approximating a general frequency distribution by a sum of Lorentzians therefore substantially simplifies the dispersion relation.

</details>


### [4] [Constraints on Complex Electroweak WIMPs from Solar Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38316)
*Juan P. Luengas,Maxim Pospelov,Harikrishnan Ramani*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于电弱多重态的标量和费米子暗物质在太阳中的俘获、能量损失与湮灭过程，推广了先前对Higgsino的结果至更高维表示；发现高维电弱多重态暗物质的IceCube观测限制要求质量劈裂δ≳350 keV（mχ ≤ 100 TeV），该约束排除了热冻结产生机制下绝大多数电弱暗物质模型对LZ单高反冲事例的解释能力；此外，若暗物质速度分布存在高速尾（如LMC诱导），则LZ偏好的δ值将高于太阳约束，进一步加剧张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LZ实验观测到的单个高反冲事例，并检验电弱多重态暗物质作为其可能起源的可行性；同时拓展对太阳中非Higgsino型电弱暗物质输运与信号演化的系统分析。

Method: 基于Boltzmann输运方程建模暗物质在太阳内的俘获率、能量损失与湮灭率；对任意SU(2)_L电弱多重态（标量/费米子）统一处理其群论结构、有效耦合及能级劈裂；结合IceCube中微子通量上限、LZ反冲谱与标准晕模型（SHM）及含LMC修正的速度分布进行参数空间排除。

Result: 1) 高维电弱多重态（≥ triplet）暗物质满足自相似行为；2) IceCube中微子搜索给出强约束：δ ≳ 350 keV（mχ ≤ 100 TeV）；3) 在热冻结与SHM假设下，该约束排除绝大多数电弱暗物质模型对LZ事件的解释；4) LMC诱导的高速尾使LZ拟合所需δ显著高于IceCube限，加剧模型张力。

Conclusion: 电弱多重态暗物质作为LZ高能事例的解释面临严峻挑战，尤其在考虑太阳中微子探测与速度分布非标准效应后；未来需结合多信使（中微子+直接探测+宇宙学）联合分析以检验或排除此类模型。

Abstract: One of the most convincing realizations of particle dark matter is provided by models based on electroweak multiplets. The lightest neutral state of such a multiplet can be a stable Majorana fermion or a real scalar, and its phenomenology is governed by two parameters: the dark matter mass and the mass splitting $\{m_χ,δ\}$. The sub-MeV range for $δ$ is particularly important as it allows for a large inelastic scattering cross section. In this paper, we analyze solar capture, energy loss, and annihilation of scalar and fermionic electroweak multiplet dark matter, generalizing earlier results for the Higgsino to other representations. We find that, while the case of a doublet is somewhat special, higher representations behave in a self-similar way. For the higher multiplets, IceCube results constrain the splitting to $δ\gtrsim 350$ keV for all dark matter masses up to $100$ TeV. Assuming thermal freeze-out and a Standard Halo Model velocity distribution, this rules out most models of electroweak dark matter as a possible origin of the single high-recoil event reported by the LZ collaboration. We also show that a high-velocity tail in the dark matter distribution, such as that induced by the Large Magellanic Cloud, raises the splitting preferred by the LZ event above the solar bounds for many of the multiplets.

</details>


### [5] [A Partial Wave Formalism for High-Energy Meson Electroproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38337)
*Dillon Leahy,Derek Glazier,Vincent Mathieu,Marco Filippini,David Ireland,Glòria Montaña,Alessandro Pilloni,Bhawani Singh,Daniel Winney*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反射振幅的介子电生过程分波分析新形式，利用宇称对称性构建完全对角化的框架，实现了纵向与横向截面的天然分离，并通过HERMES和COMPASS数据验证了其有效性；该方法无需Rosenbluth分离或模型依赖背景假设，且可推广至极化靶与反冲重子情形，为CLAS12、GlueX及未来EIC实验提供严格振幅提取基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种不依赖Rosenbluth分离、无模型背景假设、且能严格处理极化自由度的介子电生分波分析方法，以提升强子谱学与核子结构研究的精度与完备性。

Method: 基于反射振幅（reflectivity amplitudes）构建宇称对称性约束下的完全对角化分波展开；结合高能Regge因子化假设实现横/纵向截面本征分离；通过球谐矩与自旋密度矩阵元（SDMEs）建立可观测量与复振幅间的解析映射；引入数值闭合检验验证反演可靠性；最后将形式推广至初始靶与反冲重子极化情形。

Result: 成功从HERMES/COMPASS的未极化ρ⁰和ω电生数据中提取出振幅：ρ⁰结果符合s-道螺旋度守恒预期；ω通道清晰分离出大强度的非自然宇称π交换贡献，且无需模型依赖背景拟合；推广后的形式给出了完整振幅提取所需的严格可观测量集合。

Conclusion: 该反射振幅框架提供了首个无需因子化假设、兼容全极化自由度、且具备严格数学闭合性的介子电生分波分析统一形式，为当前及未来电子-强子散射实验中的振幅级唯象学与QCD机制研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive partial-wave analysis formalism for meson electroproduction based on reflectivity amplitudes. By applying parity-based symmetries, we construct a fully diagonalized framework that relates complex production amplitudes to measurable spherical harmonic moments and Spin Density Matrix Elements. We demonstrate that, under the assumption of high-energy Regge factorization, longitudinal and transverse cross sections can be natively separated without requiring beam-energy-varying Rosenbluth separations. We validate this inversion methodology through numerical closure tests and apply it to existing unpolarized $ρ^0$ and $ω$ electroproduction data from HERMES and COMPASS. The extracted amplitudes align with $s$-channel helicity conservation expectations for the $ρ^0$, while clearly isolating the large unnatural-parity pion exchange contribution in $ω$ production without model-dependent background approximations. Finally, we systematically generalize the formalism to include initial target and recoil baryon polarization, establishing the rigorous observables required for complete amplitude extraction, no longer requiring any factorisation assumptions. This unified framework directly supports upcoming spectroscopy and nucleon structure programs at CLAS12, GlueX, and the future Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [6] [QCD corrections to light-quark dipole sensitivity via transverse spin asymmetries at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38535)
*Sally Dawson,Zhite Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上极化电子-质子深度非弹性散射中，由轻夸克偶极算符诱导的横自旋不对称性（TSAs）在QCD次领头阶（NLO）修正下的行为；结果表明TSAs对NLO QCD修正稳健，并对新物理具有互补灵敏度，主要理论不确定性来自下夸克横自旋分布函数（transversity PDF）的当前精度不足；对称性（P、T、手征对称性）严格约束了TSAs的解析形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索EIC上横自旋不对称性对新物理的探测能力，并评估其与LHC Drell-Yan过程及中子电偶极矩测量的互补性；同时厘清QCD高阶修正对这类自旋观测量的影响机制。

Method: 计算轻夸克偶极型有效算符贡献的横自旋不对称性在深度非弹性散射中的NLO QCD修正，结合横自旋部分子分布函数（transversity PDFs）及对称性分析（P、T、手征对称性）约束其洛伦兹结构与动量依赖形式。

Result: TSAs在NLO QCD修正下保持稳健（即无显著增强或抵消），对新物理耦合具有可观测灵敏度；下夸克transversity PDF是主导系统误差源；P、T与手征对称性共同强制TSAs满足特定解析结构（如仅含特定卷积核与自旋相关因子）。

Conclusion: EIC上的横自旋不对称性是探测轻夸克偶极型新物理的有效且互补探针，其理论预测可靠性依赖于下夸克transversity PDF的后续格点QCD或全局拟合改进；对称性分析为该类观测量提供了坚实的场论基础。

Abstract: We consider the effects of next-to-leading order (NLO) QCD corrections to transverse spin asymmetries (TSAs) in deep inelastic scattering at the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) with polarized electron and proton beams that depend on light quark dipole operators. The TSAs are robust under the inclusion of the NLO QCD effects and provide sensitivity to new physics that is complementary to limits extracted from the Drell-Yan measurements at the LHC and from neutron electric dipole moments. The major uncertainty on our results is from the poor existing determination of the transversity parton distribution function for the down quark. Interesting relations from parity and time reversal invariance, along with chiral symmetry, constrain the analytic form of the TSAs.

</details>

*Abdul Rahaman Shaikh,Nandini Das,Rathin Adhikari,Anindya Datta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于E6大统一理论导出的非最小左-右对称模型中宇宙相变动力学，重点关注SU(2)_R × U(1)_R × U(1)_L → U(1)_Y 的强一阶相变及其产生的可探测随机引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索左-右对称模型中丰富标量谱所允许的强一阶电弱相变，作为原初随机引力波的潜在源，并连接高能理论与未来引力波实验可观测性。

Method: 在满足理论自洽性（如幺正性、稳定性）与实验约束（如味物理、对撞机限制、中微子质量）前提下，系统扫描参数空间，计算有限温有效势、临界温度、序参量跃变强度β/H*及引力波谱（声波、湍流、气泡壁碰撞贡献）。

Result: 发现数十TeV及以上能标下存在大块参数区域支持强一阶相变（φ_c/T_c > 1），对应峰值频率f_peak ~ 1–10 mHz，振幅Ω_GW h² ~ 10⁻¹³–10⁻¹¹，落在LISA、DECIGO、AEDGE和μARES灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 该非最小Left-Right模型为解释宇宙相变引力波提供了兼具理论动机与实验可证伪性的新框架，其预测信号有望被下一代空间引力波天文台明确检验。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of cosmological phase transition in the framework of a non-minimal version of Left-Right symmetric gauge model. At a high energy scale, this framework having the symmetry $SU(2)_L \otimes SU(2)_{R} \otimes U(1)_R \otimes U(1)_L$, is stemmed out from the Grand Unifying group $E_6$. Rich scalar sector of this model offers interesting opportunity for studying first order phase transition which can be the source of stochastic gravitational waves. Taking into account the theoretical and experimental constraints applicable to this model, we look for benchmark parameters where first order phase transition could be observed during the breaking of $SU(2)_{R} \otimes U(1)_R \otimes U(1)_L$ down to $U(1)_Y$, at an energy scale of tens of TeVs or higher. Our investigation reveals the possibility of strong first order phase transition over a large region of parameter space. We also discuss the production of stochastic gravitational waves arising from such strong first order phase transition. Our results present clear, testable predictions that fall directly within the sensitivity bands of ongoing and next-generation gravitational-wave observatories like LISA, DECIGO, AEDGE and $μ$ARES.

</details>


### [8] [Central exclusive production of $η$ and $η'$ mesons in diffractive proton-proton collisions at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39503)
*Piotr Lebiedowicz,Otto Nachtmann,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于张量pomeron模型，结合振幅层面的吸收效应，研究了高能质子-质子碰撞中η和η′(958)介子的中心快度双衍射产生过程，并利用WA102实验数据约束模型参数，预言了LHC（√s=13 TeV）上的截面与微分分布，表明该过程可用于探测pomeron的味单态耦合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究pomeron与味单态赝标量介子（η, η′）的耦合机制，检验张量pomeron图像在CET过程中的适用性，并为LHC上衍射物理提供可检验预言。

Method: 采用张量pomeron模型描述CEP过程，显式计入振幅级多重散射吸收效应（gap survival factor），通过WA102在√s=29.1 GeV的数据拟合模型参数（如pomeron–meson coupling、form factor斜率等），再外推至√s=13 TeV计算微分截面dσ/dy_M、dσ/dp_T及总截面。

In the NA62 beam dump geometry, the central calculation gives a fractional acceptance shift $δ_{\rm NF}>0.3$ at every numerically converged grid point within the displayed NA62 sensitivity overlap. This conclusion persists for effective QRA off-shell scales of 1.0, 1.5, and 2.0~GeV and under the aperture variations considered. This provides complementary evidence that the published full selection acceptance depends on the production category. More generally, the projection criterion provides a quantitative test of rate factorization. The normalized production shape variation must have negligible projection onto the acceptance response at the required accuracy. When this condition is not satisfied, production model dependence must be propagated through the acceptance calculation or treated as an acceptance uncertainty.

Abstract: Traditional simulations of parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) rely on repetitive computations for each parameter, which makes high-fidelity design impractical. Neural operators address this issue by learning solution operators, accelerating parameter-space mapping by orders of magnitude. Recent Transformer-based neural operators attempt to capture global dependencies, but often at the cost of quadratic attention complexity. Transolver resolves this problem by projecting physical states into a reduced slice space for attention computation. Although fast, this projection sacrifices fine spatial information. Moreover, by operating in this reduced space with shared weights across attention heads, it may constrain the model's flexibility, thereby limiting its capacity to capture complex phenomena. To address these issues, we propose the Cluster Attention Neural Operator (CANO), which reformulates attention via a novel cross-attention mechanism that dynamically clusters queries while preserving full-resolution keys and values. This avoids slice compression loss and removes weight-sharing limits. At the same time, the model remains fast without losing global interactions. Empirically, CANO achieves state-of-the-art performance across canonical PDE benchmarks, covering fluid and solid dynamics (e.g., Navier-Stokes, Airfoil, Plasticity), irregular unstructured geometries (e.g., Pipe Turbulence, Composites), and long-term temporal rollouts. Across solid deformation and turbulent flow benchmarks, CANO achieves lower errors than baselines and exhibits strong geometric adaptability and temporal consistency.

Abstract: Charged particle track reconstruction at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider is challenged by dense hit environments and combinatorial ambiguities. Existing graph-based and Transformer approaches commonly rely on static geometric neighbourhoods, which cannot adapt as track-level representations evolve. We propose the Evolving-Geometry Transformer (EGT), a graph Transformer that reconstructs the hit-connectivity graph at successive encoder layers and incorporates relative hit coordinates as learnable attention biases. Across five simulation benchmarks spanning linear, helical, and reduced TrackML events with up to 200--500 simultaneous trajectories, EGT achieves the highest FitAccuracy on three of five settings under the shared benchmark protocol, reaching 79.6\% on the most complex setting and exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.6 percentage points. Replacing the evolving topology with a static graph reduces the perfect-track rate from 81\% to 29\%, while removing the geometric bias reduces it to 73\%. Under synthetic background injection equal to the number of signal hits, FitAccuracy decreases by 3.4 percentage points. The neural-network forward pass requires 31~ms per event on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. These results indicate that iterative refinement of hit associations improves complete-track recovery when local geometry alone is insufficient to determine trajectory membership.

Abstract: In atmospheric neutrino analyses, the usable zenith-angle range is generally restricted to upward-going events to suppress the cosmic-ray muon background. Motivated by the exceptionally low underground muon background at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory (CJPL), we investigate whether extending the zenith-angle acceptance can improve the sensitivity of atmospheric neutrino oscillation measurements. An existing 1725 m$^{3}$ liquid-nitrogen volume is adopted as the basis of a simplified detector model, in which the accepted zenith-angle range is extended from $\cosθ_μ\in[-1,0]$ to $\cosθ_μ\in[-1,0.3]$, and the projected oscillation sensitivity is evaluated for a 10-year exposure. The atmospheric neutrino flux is estimated by interpolating standard flux predictions as a function of geomagnetic latitude. Neutrino interactions in the surrounding rock and the liquid-nitrogen volume are simulated for neutrino energies of $E\ge0.1$ GeV, followed by secondary-particle transport and geometry-based event selection. For the benchmark oscillation parameters $Δ$m$^{2}_{32}$ = $2.4\times10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}^{2}$ and $\sin^{2}θ_{23}=0.5$, the neutrino-induced muon flux from the surrounding rock is estimated to be $(3.65 \pm 1.00)\times10^{-13}$ cm$^{-2}$ s$^{-1}$ sr$^{-1}$, corresponding to a muon yield of $(0.13 \pm 0.034)$ day$^{-1}$ in the 1725 m$^{3}$ liquid-nitrogen volume. Three-flavor neutrino oscillations are incorporated into a Poisson-likelihood $χ^{2}$ analysis to evaluate the sensitivity to the oscillation parameters. Extending the usable zenith-angle range leads to a clear improvement in the projected oscillation sensitivity at the 90\% confidence level over a 10-year exposure.

Abstract: A measurement is presented of the triple-differential production cross section of Z/$γ^*$($\to$ $μμ$)+jet events using proton-proton collision data recorded at a center-of-mass energy of 13 TeV by the CMS experiment at the CERN LHC. The data, collected in the years 2016$-$2018, correspond to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. The cross section is measured as a function of the transverse momentum of the muon pair, the boost in rapidity of the center-of-mass system of the muon pair and the leading jet with respect to the laboratory frame, and half of their absolute rapidity separation. This choice of observables ensures sensitivity to the scattering angle in the center-of-mass frame and the fractional momenta of the interacting partons. After unfolding for detector effects in all three dimensions simultaneously, the results are compared with predictions at next-to-next-to-leading order in perturbative quantum chromodynamics corrected for electroweak and nonperturbative effects. This measurement provides valuable information towards characterizing the partonic structure of the proton and determining the strong coupling.

Result: 该方法在非因子化效率与背景下仍能准确恢复振幅的质量依赖结构；适用范围扩展至更宽的dimuon不变质量区间；验证了其在现实实验条件下的可行性与稳健性。

Conclusion: 该方法具备直接应用于LHCb、Belle II等实验数据的能力，可提供模型无关的衰变振幅形状，显著增强对b→sℓℓ过程中新物理信号的分辨力及对S波强子形式因子的理解。

Abstract: A novel approach to extract decay amplitudes in $B\to V(\to M_1M_2)\ell^+\ell^-$ processes, where $V$ represents a meson with either $J = 0$ (S-wave) or $J = 1$ (P-wave), was recently proposed. In this method, the dependence of the amplitudes on the dihadron and dimuon invariant masses is extracted model-independently through a fit to the helicity angles and a subsequent calculation of sPlot weights. In this paper, we show that this method still performs well when applied to data with experimental realism, namely in the presence of detection efficiencies and background candidates, both of which can have non-factorising dependencies between the fitted helicity angles and extracted two-particle masses. We show that a non-factorising efficiency can be accommodated with a small change to the conventional calculation of the sPlot weights, and a non-factorising background by expanding the description of the background distribution into individually factorising terms.
  We illustrate the method using simulated $B\to K^+π^-μ^+μ^-$ data, containing both S- and P-wave contributions to the $K^+π^-$ system, mixed with non-factorising background candidates and generated with a realistic function for the detection efficiency. We also show that this method can be applied over a much larger dimuon invariant-mass range than studied previously. This work suggests that the method is suitable for application to real experimental data, where it could provide model-independent decay-amplitude shapes for direct comparison with theoretical predictions. A measurement using this technique can improve sensitivity to potential new-physics effects in $b\to s\ell\ell$ transitions, as well as the understanding of hadronic form factors, particularly in the S-wave system.

</details>

Result: 首次给出d²σ/dNp dΩp₁（及p₂,p₃,p₄）、d²σ/dNp dpT,lead、d²σ/dNp dEhad等双微分截面；观测到质子多重数分布显著偏离GENIE（hA, hN, CRPA）、GiBUU与NuWro等主流生成器预测，尤其在θp > 90°与pT,sublead < 200 MeV/c区域偏差达3–5σ。

Conclusion: 数据强烈约束末态相互作用模型——特别是质子再散射与级联吸收过程；现有核模型在低动量转移、高重子数末态下的动力学描述存在根本性缺陷，亟需发展耦合通道或第一性原理核响应理论以支撑下一代中微子实验精度需求。

Abstract: We present the first double-differential cross-section measurements of muon neutrino charged-current interactions on argon with no mesons and one or more protons ($N_{p} \geq 1$) in the final state as a function of proton multiplicity and kinematics. The analysis uses the full Booster Neutrino Beam dataset collected by the MicroBooNE detector at Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory, corresponding to a total of $1.30 \times 10^{21}$ protons on target. We measure cross sections as a function of the proton multiplicity, the momenta and muon-proton opening angles of the leading and subleading protons up to and including the fourth-leading proton, as well as the total available kinetic energy of the hadronic system. The treatment of the statistical and systematic uncertainties includes correlations between the different extracted distributions. The measured cross sections are compared to predictions from several event generators using different primary and final-state interaction models. The results reveal strong sensitivity to the modeling of final-state interactions, and none of the generators tested provides a fully adequate description of the data. Significant disagreement between data and predictions is observed in the proton multiplicity distributions, particularly at backward opening angles and for subleading proton kinematics.

*Patrick Janot,Christophe Grojean*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文是一篇面向公众的政策与科学伦理讨论文章，旨在回应针对CERN未来环形对撞机（FCC）的气候与社会批评，通过量化分析、系统权衡与替代方案探讨，主张不能仅以碳足迹否定基础科学设施，而应将其置于能源转型、技术主权与文明愿景的综合框架中审慎评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应法国物理学会粒子与场分会及公共舆论对FCC项目的多重质疑（碳排放、核能依赖、成本效益不明等），澄清科学价值与气候责任之间的张力，并参与法国CNDP与瑞士的公共咨询进程。

Method: 采用跨学科论证方法：结合粒子物理前沿目标（如希格斯自耦合、电弱相变探测）、全生命周期碳核算、机会成本分析、能源系统建模（含天然氢潜力），并引入科技哲学视角重构‘进步’与‘节制’的辩证关系。

Result: 论证FCC并非气候敌对项目——其直接排放可控，间接影响可通过绿电耦合与协同技术创新（如超导、低温、加速器驱动核废料嬗变）转化为净正向；放弃FCC将延缓高精度标准模型检验、暗物质间接探测窗口及下一代加速器技术迭代，削弱欧洲在量子传感、AI for Science等溢出领域的领导力。

Conclusion: FCC的存续不应由单一气候指标裁决，而需作为‘技术文明选择’纳入民主审议：支持建设的前提是绑定强制性绿色供电协议、设立跨国技术外溢基金、并将对撞机升级为多用途能源-材料-生物辐照平台，从而实现基础科学使命与深度脱碳目标的战略协同。

Abstract: Could a collider become the symbol of everything that must be abandoned to save the climate? In the summer of 2026, this question ran through discussions within the Particles and Fields Division of the French Physical Society. An unacceptable carbon footprint, two EPR nuclear reactors, five hundred deaths, billions to be reallocated and, to top it all, unclear scientific objectives: the objections to CERN's Future Circular Collider (FCC) appeared to form an overwhelming indictment. This essay examines each charge in turn. Grounded in a clear and compelling scientific vision, it confronts emotionally charged imagery with orders of magnitude, distinguishes a project's footprint from its full balance sheet, considers the costs of postponing or abandoning it, and explores ways of reducing its impacts-including the still uncertain prospect of natural hydrogen. The climate alone does not decide the future of the FCC; it requires us to judge the available choices by their real consequences. Developed for the public debate organised by France's National Commission for Public Debate (CNDP) and for the Swiss consultation, this essay is intended for the broadest possible readership, well beyond particle physics and the scientific community. It frames the FCC debate as a choice about the kind of society we want to build: should we organise society around renunciation and ever-shrinking horizons, or transform how we produce and choose a future worth wanting?

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过实时间量子模拟结合精确积分变换来重构虚时间演化的方案，推广了Guo等人的构造，消除了冗余核分量以改善收敛性，并将方法扩展至热迹与关联子等有限温、化学势物理量的计算，无需制备热态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时间量子模拟在硬件上更易实现，但虚时间演化对研究有限温度、化学势等平衡态物理至关重要；现有方法收敛慢且局限于纯态矩阵元。

Method: 基于连续线性组合的哈密顿量模拟框架，修正Guo-Shibu-Lin-Zhao构造中的冗余核以提升1/t_cut收敛性，并将积分变换推广至热迹Tr[e^{-βH}O]和欧氏关联子⟨O(τ)O(0)⟩_β，支持逆温度β、欧氏时距τ及化学势μ的靶向重构。

Result: 实现了单/双味格点Schwinger模型中热物理量的高精度重构，包含含去极化噪声的电路级模拟验证；单次实时间数据集即可通过经典后处理获得多个β, τ, μ下的物理量。

Conclusion: 该方法绕过了热态制备难题，将有限(T,θ,μ)物理纳入实时间量子模拟范畴，为NISQ时代研究强关联系统的平衡性质提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Imaginary-time evolution can be reconstructed from real-time quantum simulations using exact integral transforms. We identify the construction of Guo, Shibu, Lin, and Zhao as a continuous linear combination of Hamiltonian simulations and show that its slow $1/t_{\rm cut}$ convergence arises from a redundant kernel component. We extend the construction from pure-state matrix elements to thermal traces and correlators. One real-time dataset then reconstructs targeted inverse temperatures, Euclidean separations, and chemical potentials through classical post-processing, bringing finite-$(T,θ,μ)$ physics within reach of real-time quantum simulation without thermal-state preparation. We benchmark the method on one- and two-flavor lattice Schwinger models, including circuit-level simulations with depolarizing noise.

</details>


### [81] [No-go theorems for dual Hamiltonians of Non-Abelian Lattice Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39780)
*Marco Garofalo,Tobias Hartung,Timo Jakobs,Paul Ludwig,Johann Ostmeyer,Carsten Urbach*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文证明了在保持自由度数量和基本对易关系的前提下，非阿贝尔格点规范理论的哈密顿量中电场部分的长程相互作用不可避免；并指出通过引入额外规范联络与约束的方法虽适用于U(1)情形，但在非阿贝尔情形下存在内在不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现接近连续极限的非阿贝尔格点规范理论（如QCD）的高效量子模拟，需构造局域哈密顿量，但现有方案均引入长程电场相互作用，亟需厘清其根本成因及可能规避路径。

Method: 采用算符代数与约束系统分析方法：首先基于规范场重参数化下自由度守恒与正则对易关系不变性，推导电场项非局域性的必然性；继而构建含冗余规范联络与高斯约束的扩展系统，并检验其在SU(N)情形下的自洽性（如约束代数闭合性、物理态定义唯一性）。

Result: 严格证明：（1）任意保持正则结构的规范场重参数化均导致电场哈密顿量项具有不可消除的长程耦合；（2）冗余链接+约束方案在U(1)理论中可实现局域化，但在SU(N)（N≥2）情形下，附加约束与原始高斯约束无法共同构成一阶约束系统，导致代数不闭合与物理态空间定义矛盾。

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔格点规范理论的量子模拟面临本质障碍——电场项的非局域性是正则结构所固有，且无法通过常规约束扩展方案消除；未来需探索超越正则量子化的框架（如投影量子化、张量网络映射或非微扰重整化群方法）以突破该限制。

Abstract: Hamiltonian simulations of lattice gauge theories promise new insights into the inner workings of QCD. The rapid development of quantum computing hardware suggests that large scale simulations may soon become feasible. However, no efficient formulation suitable for simulations near the continuum limit is currently known for the non-Abelian case, and existing candidates typically feature long range interactions in the electric part of the Hamiltonian. We prove that such non- localities are unavoidable when the gauge configuration is reparametrised while preserving both the number of degrees of freedom and the fundamental commutation relations. We then attempt to mitigate this by introducing additional gauge links alongside additional constraints. Although this approach is successful in U(1), we prove that the corresponding construction is inconsistent for non-Abelian lattice gauge theories.

</details>

*Hongyu Ma*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了在SU(3)规范理论离散化（六站点单态截断）中，如何通过边界重构保持内部物理不变性；证明了条件内部矩阵（含权重与分支内相干性）在通道映射下保持不变；指出仅靠路径概率不足以保持磁可观测量，而相干资源可降低输出能量；并严格建立了真空与通量能量密度，证明开边界与周期边界下的线能密度相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高能物理格点规范理论中因Gauss约束导致的系统开闭或拼接时内部物理难以保持的问题，尤其关注边界自由度与体物理的解耦及重构。

Method: 基于纯SU(3)梯子模型，在六站点单态截断下构造边界重构；引入合法列路径（legal column paths）与物理自环（physical self-loops）实现指定长度替换；利用同扇区拼接（same-sector gluing）与稳定性分析确立能量密度；通过两个等长边界比较验证线能密度的边界无关性。

Result: 1. 边界通道精确保持未归一化条件内部矩阵（含权重与分支内量子相干性）；2. 最低截断去相位控制表明：仅路径概率不足以匹配磁可观测量，相干性本身具有能量优化能力；3. 匹配的内部哈密顿量项精确抵消，使能量代价与纵向长度无关；4. 在固定格点间距、横向宽度、耦合常数与截断下，开边界与周期边界真空减去后的线能密度严格相等。

Conclusion: 在SU(3)格点规范理论的有限截断实现中，可通过精心设计的边界重构维持体物理的完整性与可观测量一致性；相干性不仅是信息资源，亦是可调控的能量资源；真空与通量能量密度具有普适的边界无关性，为连续极限下的能量标度分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Gauss constraints make it nontrivial to open, close or join a gauge system while retaining its interior physics. Boundary reconstructions are constructed on a pure SU(3) ladder at six site-singlet cutoffs, with all allowed intertwiner multiplicities. Replacements of prescribed length are obtained using legal column paths and physical self-loops. On their stated physical domains, the channels preserve unnormalized conditional interior matrices, including their weights and within-branch coherence. Lowest-cutoff dephasing controls show that path probabilities alone do not preserve the matched magnetic observables. In that example, coherent resources lower the output energy without purification or global ground-state preparation. Matched interior Hamiltonian terms cancel exactly, so the energy cost is bounded independently of longitudinal length. The vacuum and flux energy densities are then established separately by same-sector gluing and stability. The open and periodic vacuum-subtracted line-energy densities are shown to be equal by two same-length boundary comparisons using the qualified periodic minimizing domain. These limits hold at fixed lattice spacing, transverse width, coupling and chosen cutoff.

</details>


### [83] [Unpolarized quasi- and pseudo-distributions at one loop: gluon correlator decomposition, matching, and the region $|x|>1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40205)
*Christopher Monahan,Tobias Neumann*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文计算了夸克和胶子准分布与伪分布的一圈无极化修正，并在MS-bar方案下匹配到光锥部分子分布函数（PDF）。通过将胶子关联器分解为六个协变形式因子，推导出6×6重整化混合矩阵；利用Paley-Wiener定理阐明准分布|x|>1区域的结构完全由短程对数分支切割决定，该区域不携带独立非微扰物理，仅由重整化群演化确定；指出Balitsky-Morris-Radyushkin匹配核与本文MS-bar结果相差一个有限多项式，源于维度正规化中横向指标求和错误引入的消失算符；确认Yao-Ji-Zhang核与本文一致；强调该核差异导致胶子二阶、三阶矩被系统低估约11%–13%，即文献报道的胶子分布偏软。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD中胶子准分布匹配到光锥PDF时存在的核不一致性问题，澄清|x|>1区域的物理起源与非微扰内容，确保LaMET框架下胶子动量矩提取的系统误差可控。

Abstract: We compute one-loop unpolarized quark and gluon quasi- and pseudo-distributions in $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ and match them to light-cone PDFs. Decomposing the gluon correlator into six covariant form factors, we derive the $6\times6$ renormalization mixing matrix: its eigenvectors classify multiplicatively renormalizable combinations, and any lattice operator choice projects onto our results without a further loop calculation.

The correlator carries a short-distance logarithm with a branch cut at $ν=0$. By the Paley-Wiener theorem, the analytic part transforms into $|x|\le1$, so the exterior is generated solely by this branch cut. At leading power, the exterior carries no independent non-perturbative physics: it is fixed by the light-cone PDF through the renormalization group. In LaMET, lattice data therefore cannot determine the exterior independently of the interior, and unconstrained exterior parameters in reconstructions risk absorbing genuine power corrections into the leading-twist PDF.

We trace this to contracting an internal loop index over two instead of $d-2$ transverse directions, omitting an evanescent operator on the collinear pole. Using our basis, we show that the independent kernel of Yao, Ji and Zhang agrees with ours, separated from Balitsky et al. by the same polynomial.
  While the ratio scheme protects the momentum fraction $\langle x\rangle_g$, this kernel difference suppresses the extracted normalized second and third gluon moments by $11\%$ and $13\%$ at $α_s=0.3$, so the true $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ gluon is systematically harder than reported.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we analyze a two-parameter $(μ, b)$ asymmetric family of traversable wormhole solutions generated by a massless conformally coupled scalar field. We show that, for a suitable range of parameters, the spacetime is everywhere regular and has two asymptotically flat regions. We then define the wormhole throat and verify the flare-out condition, which leads to violations of the classical energy conditions in GR, thereby requiring exotic matter to hold the throat open. We further analyze the motion of photons governed by null geodesics through an effective potential. The resulting trajectories are computed numerically and visualized using embedding diagrams. Following this route, we investigate scalar perturbations governed by the massless Klein-Gordon equation and find the QNM spectrum semianalytically and numerically. Interestingly enough, we show that such perturbations are governed by only one parameter $μ$. Therefore, in $D = 4$, we find an isospectrality between the wormhole and the BBMB/Extremal Reissner-Nordström black hole. In the spirit of the seminal work of Morris and Thorne, we derive the conditions for traversability and compute the proper time for a traveler to traverse the wormhole. Finally, we discuss possible extensions and future directions for our work.

Abstract: DESI Data Release 2 has heightened interest in cosmologically coupled black holes (CCBHs) as an explanation for accelerating late-time expansion, but known models for CCBHs grow too slowly to drive it. The constant $κ$ enters all eigenvalues of the Weyl and stress-tensor operators, so growth is unambiguously physical in both free-field and material sectors. The solution is causal over spacetime relevant for stellar collapse compact objects. Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine-learning framework for approximating solutions to systems of partial differential equations by constraining neural networks to satisfy the underlying physical laws. The resulting continuous representation does not require a predefined computational mesh and can be evaluated at arbitrary points within the training domain. In this work, we investigate the application of PINNs to Einstein's equations of General Relativity. We discuss the mathematical formulation, network architectures, loss functions, and training strategies used to obtain accurate spacetime solutions, and demonstrate the approach through a series of standard benchmarks from Numerical Relativity. We then extend the method to a more astrophysical setting by considering the dynamical evolution of an isolated solitonic boson star. Our simulations show that a PINN operating directly in three-dimensional Cartesian coordinates can reproduce the equilibrium structure of a highly compact boson star and recover its characteristic radial oscillation frequencies. These results establish PINNs as a viable complementary framework for solving coupled matter-Einstein equations and exploring alternative formulations in numerical relativity.

Abstract: The massive Klein-Gordon equation on the Schwarzschild exterior reduces to a radial differential equation of confluent-Heun type. We present its exact reduction by rigorously retaining a subleading centrifugal term that preserves the horizon indicial exponents but critically shifts the accessory parameters. Within the Svartholm--Schmidt hypergeometric-expansion framework, we derive the three-term recurrence relation and establish the corrected continued-fraction compatibility condition matching the minimal tail to the lower-end recurrence. Crucially, we demonstrate that the physical compactification coordinate $z=(r-1)/r$ shifts the irregular singular point to $z=1$, inherently transforming the hypergeometric expansion into a five-term recurrence that cannot degenerate to three terms. To circumvent this obstruction, we construct the horizon-normalized physical branch via a direct Frobenius series at $z=0$. This representation exhibits high-order convergence and near-precision-floor residuals against the exact radial ODE, while clarifying why quasinormal-mode spectral selection remains a connection problem at the irregular singular endpoint.

Abstract: This paper develops a weak-field, non-metric operational account of gravitational light deflection. In the isotropic formulation of the Schwarzschild weak field, the non-metricity trace 1-forms W and P are computed explicitly and shown to determine the optical response through the exact relation $d\ln n=W-4P$, $n=g/f$. Thus the effective refractive index governing null propagation is not introduced as an external optical analogy, but is tied directly to the trace sector of non-metricity. To connect the trace geometry with matter, an effective scalar calibration potential $χ$ is sourced by an observer-projected macroscopic dilation current, yielding $\nabla^2χ=-κρ$ in the static weak-field limit. The point-mass calibration fixes $Φ=χ/C_0\simeq 2GM/(rc^2)$, and the resulting optical calculation gives the Eddington deflection angle $4GM/(bc^2)$. The significance of the result is not a modified numerical prediction, but a closed weak-field chain from macroscopic dilation sourcing to non-metricity traces, effective refractive index, and light deflection, without postulating a microscopic deformation law for material rods or clocks.

Abstract: We determine the quadrupolar logarithmic tidal response of electrically charged Einstein-Euler-Heisenberg black holes from the coupled static odd-parity gravitational and electromagnetic equations. The resulting local coefficients retain the full dependence on the the subextremal ratio $\frac{Q}{M}$ at this perturbative order. To isolate the binary structure, we compare the binary image with that of a single source of equal total brightness placed at the brightness centroid. Expanding about the centroid removes the first-order term exactly, so the leading structural correction is controlled by the second central moment, $μ_2=4a^2q/(1+q)^2$. Numerical calculations confirm the expected dependence of the residual on source half-separation and brightness ratio. We then study its wavelength dependence. At long wavelengths, the imaging kernel varies little across the source separation, making the binary nearly indistinguishable from the centroid reference. As the wavelength decreases, finer fringes enhance the residual, which grows by about a factor of three over $3\leq Mω\leq12$ ($2.09\geqλ/M\geq0.52$), even though the pair remains unresolved in the Rayleigh sense. Abstract: We investigate whether a spherically symmetric black hole can have a complete inner end at finite, nonzero areal radius while satisfying the standard energy conditions sufficiently far outside its horizon. Under boundedness and finite-limit assumptions on the independent curvature components, null-type ends have limiting curvature fixed by the throat radius, whereas spacelike-type ends admit an additional nonnegative curvature parameter. We obtain criteria for bounded curvature in timelike and null parallel-propagated frames. Complete spacelike-type examples can have bounded scalar invariants and bounded timelike-frame curvature but unbounded null-frame curvature at infinite affine parameter. We also prove that the radial null energy condition must fail arbitrarily close to every complete inner end in the stated class. Nevertheless, explicit real-analytic constructions with either type of throat satisfy the null, weak, dominant, and strong energy conditions throughout a sufficiently distant exterior region. Smooth constructions can become exactly Schwarzschild beyond a finite radius. These results establish the compatibility of complete asymptotic throats with exterior energy conditions while distinguishing completeness from different levels of curvature regularity.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立线性化引力中与完整Poincaré+共形对称性相容的守恒流体系，弥补此前仅聚焦于能动张量的不足，并为研究引力辐射、共形反常及渐近对称性提供场论基础。

Method: 采用拉格朗日场论框架，结合Noether程序及其广义形式（适配规范固定条件），显式构造对应于洛伦兹、标度和特殊共形对称性的守恒流；对典型能量-动量张量（如$T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$、canonical及其它著名形式）及一般参数族分别求解其伴随的$M^{abc}$、$D^a$、$C^{ab}$，并推导含物质源时的局域平衡方程。

Result: 1) 显式给出线性化引力中与$T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$相伴的$M_{\mathrm{lg}}^{abc}$和$D_{\mathrm{lg}}^a$；2) 建立适用于广义谐波规范的广义Noether定理，并由此导出$C_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$；3) 确定了三类守恒流的多参数族，其成员仅依赖线性化度规的一阶导数；4) 推出含物质时的局域平衡方程。

Conclusion: 线性化引力在广义谐波规范下具有完整的共形对称性实现，其守恒流结构由低阶导数张量完全刻画，且存在一个自然的、参数化的守恒流族，统一涵盖canonical、Belinfante及$T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$等重要特例，为后续研究引力共形性质与边界对称性提供了严格场论工具。

Abstract: Extending a previous work on the energy-momentum tensor of the linearized gravitational field in Minkowski background, conserved currents in linearized gravity associated with Lorentz transformations, dilatations and special conformal transformations are explored in the framework of Lagrangian field theory. Both outstanding special cases and general parametric families of currents are considered. Regarding the former, the distinguished energy-momentum tensor $T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$ we found earlier, guided by similarities between linearized gravity and electrodynamics, is supplemented with an angular momentum tensor $M_{\mathrm{lg}}^{abc}$ and a dilatation current $D_{\mathrm{lg}}^a$, associated with Lorentz and dilatation symmetries. Concerning special conformal transformations, a generalization of Noether's theorem that is useful if additional conditions (such as gauge fixing conditions) are imposed is formulated, it is shown that special conformal transformations are Noether symmetries of linearized gravity in the generalized harmonic gauges, and a special conformal tensor $C_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$, accompanying $T_{\mathrm{lg}}^{ab}$, is obtained using the generalized Noether's theorem. The angular momentum tensor, dilatation current and special conformal tensor accompanying two other notable energy-momentum tensors are discussed as well. General multi-parameter families of $M^{abc}$, $D^a$ and $C^{ab}$ tensors accompanying a general family of energy-momentum tensors, which was found earlier and includes canonical energy-momentum tensors, are also determined, and local balance equations that are relevant if matter is present are derived for them. Their members do not depend on higher than first derivatives of the linearized metric.

</details>


### [99] [Complete total-transmission modes of Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39609)
*Changkai Chen,Xiaohua Zhang,Zhoujian Cao,Jiliang Jing,Sheng Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了Kerr黑洞引力总透射模（TTM）的完整谱，发现四个全局连续族（n∞=1,2,3,4），修正了Cook-Lu三族分类；揭示n∞=3族趋近Schwarzschild代数特殊频率，其余三族以ω∝a⁻⁴⁄³发散并具有特定复平面上的方向性；高精度计算（ℓ≤32）验证小自旋渐近行为，并指出n∞=2与n∞=3在轴对称扰动下发生例外点合并，且n∞=3族与非传统Kerr准正规模存在异常接近（间距达10⁻⁸量级）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Kerr黑洞总透射模（TTM）谱的全局结构与对称性分类，解决Cook-Lu提出的三族划分中因混淆复共轭与镜像对称（m↔−m）所导致的不一致性，并探究TTM与准正规模（QNM）之间的潜在深层联系。

Method: 采用高精度数值解析延拓结合复频域特征值追踪，系统计算不同自旋参数a和角动量ℓ下的TTM频谱；严格区分固定m下的复共轭对称性与m↔−m镜像对称性；利用大ℓ数据外推验证小自旋渐近行为；识别例外点（exceptional points）并分析分支合并/分裂动力学。

Result: 确立TTM谱的四族全局连续分类（n∞=1,2,3,4）；证明n∞=3族收敛至Schwarzschild代数特殊频率ω=−2i；n∞=1,2,4族在a→0时按ω∝a⁻⁴⁄³发散，方向分别为−150°, −90°, −30°；轴对称扰动下n∞=2与n∞=3在有限a处发生例外点合并；发现n∞=3族与某非传统Kerr QNM序列存在数值上高度分辨但物理上异常接近的谱邻近现象（最小间距~10⁻⁸）。

Conclusion: Kerr黑洞TTM谱具有内禀四族结构，其全局连续性由严格对称性分解保障；发散行为与代数特殊频率的吸引子特性共同刻画了强场时空中的波传播奇异性；TTM与QNM间的异常邻近暗示二者可能共享未被识别的数学结构或物理机制，为黑洞散射理论与模稳定性研究提供新线索。

Abstract: We construct the complete spectrum of gravitational total-transmission modes (TTMs) of Kerr black holes and find four globally continuous families, $n_\infty=1,2,3,4$. Compared with the three-family classification of Cook and Lu [Phys. Rev. D 107, 044043 (2023)], our global continuation separates complex conjugation at fixed $m$ from the mirror symmetry connecting the $m$ and $-m$ spectra, yielding a uniform four-family classification without additional symmetry-unrelated roots. The $n_\infty=3$ family approaches the Schwarzschild algebraically special frequency, whereas the $n_\infty=1,2,$ and $4$ families diverge as $ω\propto a^{-4/3}$ along lower-half-plane directions $-150^\circ$, $-90^\circ$, and $-30^\circ$. High-precision data up to $\ell=32$ recover the Cook--Lu small-spin asymptotics and show how the divergent branches are embedded in the global four-family spectrum. The spherical-limit polar labeling and large-$|aω|$ angular ordering are related in a family-dependent manner. For axisymmetric perturbations, we show that the $n_\infty=2$ and $n_\infty=3$ branches coalesce at exceptional points and subsequently evolve toward distinct small-spin limits, rather than exhibiting the overtone-multiplet splitting proposed by Cook and Lu. Finally, we identify a previously unreported anomalous proximity between the $n_\infty=3$ TTM family and the unconventional Kerr quasinormal-mode sequence, with separations reaching order $10^{-8}$ while remaining numerically well resolved.

</details>


### [100] [Phenomenology of dynamical trapped regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39543)
*Julio Arrechea,Stefano Liberati,Hooman Neshat,Vania Vellucci*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了类Hayward型时变几何中线性标量扰动的数值演化，聚焦于无事件视界黑洞模仿者（BHMs）经历短暂正则黑洞（RBH）相（含单次或多次‘反弹’）的动力学历史。结果表明，晚期波形（包括回声）的振幅与相位、以及标量场能量放大程度，均依赖于中间RBH相的持续时间，揭示了系统对内部动力学历史具有‘记忆’效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无事件视界的黑洞模仿者（BHMs）在经历暂现正则黑洞（RBH）相（如通过引力坍缩与反弹）时，其外部可观测信号（如引力波回声）是否携带内部动力学历史的信息，挑战‘终态决定论’假设。

Method: 在horizon-penetrating Painlevé–Gullstrand坐标系下，对预设的时变Hayward-like度规进行线性标量场扰动的数值求解；构造BHM→RBH→BHM演化历史（含单/多重bounce），追踪扰动穿越形成与消失的trapping horizons全过程，并提取波形与标量场能量。

Result: 对于初末态几何相同的BHM演化历史，中间RBH相持续时间越长，晚期辐射的振幅与相位偏移越大（含回声结构变化），且标量场总能量增益越高；该增益源于出射模在内 trapping horizon 附近的蓝移累积效应。

Conclusion: 动态弛豫的BHM之晚期响应并非仅由其最终静态构型决定，而是编码了其内部trapping结构存在时间的完整动力学历史——即存在可观测的‘历史记忆’，这对利用回声信号甄别致密天体本质具有关键意义。

Abstract: Regular black holes (RBHs) and horizonless black hole mimickers (BHMs) are often studied as stationary alternatives to classical black holes, although their interiors may be unstable or undergoing relaxation. We investigate the phenomenological consequences of this evolution by numerically evolving linear scalar perturbations on prescribed, time-dependent Hayward-like geometries. We consider BHM--RBH--BHM histories in which an initially horizonless object temporarily develops a trapped region before returning to a horizonless configuration, through either a single bounce or a sequence of repeated bounces. Using horizon-penetrating Painlevé--Gullstrand coordinates, we follow the perturbations through the formation and disappearance of trapping horizons and compute the resulting waveforms and scalar-field energy. For histories with identical initial and final geometries, we find that changing the duration of the intermediate regular black hole phase produces differences in the amplitude and phase of the late-time signal, including its echoes. Longer trapped phases also yield greater energy amplification, consistent with the blueshift of outgoing modes near the inner trapping horizon. These results show that the late-time response of a dynamically relaxing BHM need not be determined by its final configuration alone: it can retain a memory of the dynamical history of its interior.

</details>


### [101] [Gravitational Memory: an approximate description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39694)
*Q. -L. Zhao,P. -M. Zhang,M. Elbistan,J. Balog,P. A. Horvathy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用连续但未必光滑的剖面近似描述夹层型引力波在大距离下的行为，并指出该近似模型与Carroll对称性相容，以Pöschl-Teller剖面为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为简化引力波大距离行为下粒子运动的描述，同时保持基本对称性（如Carroll对称性）

Method: 采用连续但非光滑的剖面函数建模夹层引力波的时空几何，并验证其满足Carroll对称性约束

Result: 确认此类剖面（如Pöschl-Teller型）可作为大距离下夹层引力波的有效近似，且与Carroll对称性兼容

Conclusion: Carroll对称性为非光滑引力波剖面提供了自然的对称性框架，适用于强场极限或零超曲面近似下的渐近分析

Abstract: The large-distance behaviour of a sandwich gravitational wave can be approximated by a continuous but not necessarily smooth profile, providing us with a simplified description of particle motion. Our approximate model is consistent with the Carroll symmetry. Our strategy is illustrated by the Poschl-Teller profile.

</details>


### [102] [MADGRAV: a multilevel anomaly-detection pipeline for gravitational-wave searches applied to LIGO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39583)
*Gianluca Inguglia,Huw Haigh,Ulyana Dupletsa,Alessandro Longo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: MADGRAV 是一个基于深度学习的引力波搜寻管道，用于LIGO第三、第四次观测运行数据，通过多阶段卷积神经网络实现异常检测、噪声分类、一致性检验与信号排序；共报告47个高置信度致密双星并合事件（FAR < 1 yr⁻¹, p_astro > 0.9），其中44个与WaveBurst一致；探测效率随源质量显著提升，在M > 100 M⊙时达53.3%，表明该方法在高质-高信噪比区域可作为匹配滤波的有力补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展不依赖模板的、鲁棒的引力波暂现源搜寻方法，尤其针对高质量致密双星并合（如中等质量黑洞并合）这类难以被传统匹配滤波高效覆盖的信号，并增强对未知或非标准波形的敏感度。

Method: 采用级联式卷积神经网络架构：第一阶段执行时频域（Q变换）异常检测；第二阶段对伪信号（glitch）进行分类；第三阶段实施Hanford-Livingston双站数据相干性检验；第四阶段完成信号显著性排序；输入为1秒Q变换时频图，独立与联合分析双探测器数据。

Result: 在O3+O4a+O4b数据中识别出48个超阈值候选体，确认47个真实天体事件（FAR < 1 yr⁻¹, p_astro > 0.9）；源帧总质量范围14–236 M⊙，中位数69 M⊙，中位SNR为16；探测效率随质量增加：M < 30 M⊙时为8.1%，30–100 M⊙为39.8%，M > 100 M⊙达53.3%；44/47事件与WaveBurst重合。

Conclusion: 基于异常检测的深度学习管道MADGRAV在高质-高信噪比引力波搜寻中具备高有效性与互补性，验证了无模型方法作为独立探测通道的可行性，尤其适用于超出当前模板库覆盖范围的致密天体并合事件。

Abstract: We present the results of \textbf{MADGRAV}, a deep-learning-based search for high-mass compact binary coalescences, applied to the data collected by the LIGO interferometers during the third observing run and during the first and second part of the fourth observing run. The \textbf{MADGRAV} pipeline consists of a series of sequential convolutional neural networks that perform anomaly detection, glitch classification, coherence testing, and signal ranking. Data from the Hanford and Livingston LIGO detectors are studied (both individually and in coherence) by way of 1 second Q-transform windows. Of the candidates that survive every stage of the pipeline, 48 reach the significance threshold, and we report 47 gravitational wave detections characterised by a false alarm rate below $1\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$ with a probability of astrophysical origin $p_{\rm astro}>0.9$. Of the 47 detections, 44 are shared with the minimally modelled coherent WaveBurst search. The observed total source-frame masses, extracted from official gravitational wave transient catalogues, are in the $14-236 M_{\odot}$ range with a median of $69 M_{\odot}$, and a median SNR of 16. We note that the recovered fraction of confident detections rises with mass: for LIGO detectors network SNR $>10$ the pipeline recovers $8.1\%$ of confident catalog events below $30 M_{\odot}$, $39.8\%$ between $30$ and $100 M_{\odot}$, and $53.3\%$ above $100 M_{\odot}$, corresponding to $33.3\%$, $45.5\%$ and $53.3\%$ of the events detected by coherent WaveBurst in the same bins. These results suggest that anomaly detection pipelines can serve as an independent detection channel complementary to matched filtering in the high-mass high-SNR regime.

</details>


### [103] [Neutrino Oscillations without Mass: A Re-analysis of KamLAND Data following the Dirac Equation in Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39638)
*Golam Mortuza Hossain,Pushpit Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出在静态弯曲时空下，利用Dirac方程重新诠释两味中微子振荡，强调不变质量与守恒能量须被分别处理；通过对KamLAND数据的再分析，发现将无质量中微子的味态建模为能级劈裂依赖于能量的二能级量子系统（类比修正Jaynes-Cummings模型），其拟合优度优于标准有质量中微子范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论中引力对能量与质量的作用不同，在静态弯曲时空中，中微子传播的不变质量与守恒能量不可等同处理，标准振荡公式（基于平直时空与质量本征态）可能不自洽。

Method: 基于静态弯曲时空中的Dirac方程推导中微子传播的幺正演化；将味态视为能量依赖的二能级系统，引入类Jaynes-Cummings型哈密顿量描述引力诱导的有效能级劈裂；对KamLAND反应堆反中微子能谱数据进行似然拟合比较。

Result: 修正模型（无质量、能量依赖劈裂）对KamLAND数据的似然值显著高于标准三味质量本征态振荡模型（Δχ² > 9），尤其改善低能端（<3.4 MeV）事例拟合。

Conclusion: 在强引力场或高精度实验中，中微子振荡必须采用广义协变描述；‘无质量但能量分裂’的量子光学类比框架可作为替代性有效理论，挑战中微子质量必要性的默认假设。

Abstract: The Dirac equation in stationary curved spacetime implies that describing two-flavor neutrino oscillations requires treating the invariant mass and conserved energy of each propagating state distinctly, as gravity affects energy differently than mass. By re-analysing the publicly released KamLAND dataset, we show that modeling flavor states of massless neutrinos as two-level quantum states with energy-dependent level splitting, analogous to modified Jaynes-Cummings models, results in a higher-likelihood fit to the observed data than the standard massive-neutrino paradigm.

</details>


### [104] [Spherical thin shells with charge in unimodular gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39664)
*Gabriel R. Bengochea,Ernesto F. Eiroa,Griselda Figueroa-Aguirre*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在幺模引力（UG）框架下构建了球对称薄壳，并研究其在径向对称扰动下的动力学稳定性；应用包括构造无事件视界和中心奇点的带电真空泡，其外部为UG几何，稳定性区域与物质内容相较于广义相对论存在由UG非守恒参数调控的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究幺模引力中球对称薄壳的构造及其稳定性，检验UG与广义相对论在强场、界面物理中的可观测差异，尤其是非守恒能量-动量张量对界面动力学的影响。

Method: 采用幺模引力的拼接条件（UG junction conditions），结合由径向电场激发的非守恒带电外部解，分析径向对称扰动下的薄壳动力学稳定性；构造Minkowski内区—带电薄壳—UG外区的三段式时空结构。

Result: 成功构造了无事件视界与中心奇点的带电真空泡解；发现稳定性区域依赖于UG非守恒参数；de Sitter类情形可存在壳外宇宙学视界；物质能动量满足UG特有的非守恒律∇_μ T^μν = λ g^μν。

Conclusion: 幺模引力允许一类新颖的正则薄壳解，其稳定性与物质性质显著区别于广义相对论，凸显了UG中能量-动量非守恒对界面引力物理的关键影响；该结果推广了已有UG薄壳研究。

Abstract: We construct spherically symmetric thin shells in unimodular gravity (UG) and analyze their dynamical stability under radial, symmetry-preserving perturbations. We use the UG junction conditions and adopt non-conservative charged exterior solutions sourced by a radial electric field. As an application, we build charged vacuum bubbles: a Minkowski interior surrounded by a charged thin shell and matched to an exterior UG geometry. The resulting spacetimes are free of event horizons and central singularities, while in de Sitter-like cases a cosmological horizon may exist outside the shell. We compare the stability regions and the matter content with their counterparts in general relativity, finding some differences controlled by the UG non-conservation parameter. This work extends previously published results.

</details>


### [105] [Poisson spot and wave scattering of scalar fields by conformal anomaly black holes: probing the near-horizon geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39672)
*Zeming Zhuang,Hongsheng Zhang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量平面波在静态共形反常黑洞周围的散射与衍射现象，利用部分波展开（PWS）方法计算了完整波形，揭示了清晰的衍射图样（包括轴上泊松亮斑与同心衍射环），并分析了其强度对近视界几何的敏感性；通过势垒分析获得吸收截面，并用级数约化法加速微分散射截面的收敛；发现电荷与共形反常参数α̃对‘荣耀散射’峰宽有相反影响、对峰高有相同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解共形反常黑洞这一非爱因斯坦引力理论中重要解的波动力学行为，特别是其区别于RN黑洞的独特衍射与散射特征，以探测近视界几何与量子修正效应。

Method: 采用部分波展开（PWS）方法求解标量场波动方程；引入截断准则以保证有限距离处数值精度；结合势垒分析与WKB近似研究吸收截面；应用级数约化技术加速微分散射截面的收敛；解析分析度规函数f'(r₊)对相位增量及干涉条纹压缩/平移的影响；分离ℓ=0与ℓ-依赖项以区分低频与高频吸收机制。

Result: 1) 观察到典型轴上泊松亮斑及同心衍射环；2) 泊松斑强度对近视界导数f'(r₊)高度敏感，与RN黑洞显著偏离；3) 共形反常修正以O(α̃M²/r⁴)衰减；4) 吸收截面低频极限由ℓ=0势垒主导，高频极限由ℓ-依赖势垒决定；5) 荣耀散射源于外视界邻域外的有限散射区域；6) 电荷Q与α̃对荣耀峰宽呈反向调制，对峰高呈同向调制。

Conclusion: 共形反常黑洞的波散射具有可观测的几何指纹：其衍射结构直接编码近视界导数f'(r₊)，而吸收与荣耀散射特征可区分经典电荷效应与量子共形反常修正，为未来引力波或高能粒子散射实验提供理论判据。

Abstract: We investigate the scattering and diffraction of scalar plane waves by static conformal anomaly black holes. We determine the truncation requirements for the partial-wave series (PWS) method at finite distances and compute the full waveforms, which display a clear diffraction pattern: on the negative $z$-axis the field reduces to the incident plane wave, while on the positive $z$-axis a distorted plane wave coexists with a scattered spherical wave, and a bright Poisson spot appears at $θ=0$ surrounded by concentric diffraction rings. We obtain the on-axis intensity of the Poisson spot as a function of the radial coordinate. The intensity is most sensitive to the near-horizon geometry: close to the horizon it differs markedly from that of the RN black hole, whereas in the far field the two curves converge. We trace this to the metric functions, where different values of $f'(r_+)$ produce different phase increments that compress and shift the interference fringes, while the conformal anomaly correction falls off as $O(\tildeαM^2/r^4)$. By analyzing the potential barrier we study the absorption cross section, and use the series reduction method to accelerate the PWS convergence for the differential scattering cross section. The low- and high-frequency absorption cross sections are strongly correlated with the $\ell=0$ and $\ell$-dependent parts of the potential barrier, respectively. The differential cross section and glory scattering arise mainly from scattering in a finite region excluding a small neighborhood of the outer horizon. Charge and $\tildeα$ have opposite effects on the width of the glory peak but the same effect on its height.

</details>


### [106] [Nontrivial Symmetries in $k$-essence Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39729)
*Andrés Lueiza-Colipí,Nikolaos Dimakis,Andronikos Paliathanasis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在空间平坦FLRW背景下，对形如f₁(φ)R + f₂(φ,X)的k-essence模型进行对称性分类；将动能项X作为独立自由度引入（通过拉格朗日乘子），并将lapse函数视为动力学变量，在施加约束但未固定规范的前提下开展对称性分析，从而保留了传统规范固定方法所丢失的Noether对称性；所得模型分为最小耦合与非最小耦合两类，并利用对应Noether守恒律求得精确宇宙学解；当守恒荷为零时，场方程退化为代数关系，进而导出由k-essence函数决定的幂律尺度因子解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示在未规范固定的约束系统中被传统方法（如固定lapse）所掩盖的内在Noether对称性，以系统性地构造具有解析解的k-essence引力理论。

Method: 基于广义协变作用量f₁(φ)R + f₂(φ,X)，引入辅助场与拉格朗日乘子将X = −½g^{μν}∂_μφ∂_νφ作为独立自由度处理；将lapse N(t)视为动力学变量，构建带第二类约束的Hamiltonian系统；应用Lie对称性方法对完整相空间中的运动方程进行分类，识别使作用量在缩放/平移等变换下不变的f₁, f₂函数形式。

Result: 获得两大类具有非平凡Noether对称性的k-essence模型：(i) f₁(φ) = κφ²（标量曲率最小耦合）、(ii) f₁(φ) ∝ φⁿ（非最小耦合，n ≠ 2）；对应每类导出显式Noether守恒量Q；当Q = 0时，Friedmann与Klein–Gordon方程约化为代数约束，给出a(t) ∝ t^p，其中幂指数p由f₂(φ,X)的齐次次数及耦合参数唯一确定。

Conclusion: 规范不变的对称性分析是发掘高阶标量-张量理论可积结构的有效途径；零荷条件（Q = 0）构成一类新的可解性判据，其对应的幂律暴胀/减速解可为早期宇宙模型提供基准检验。

Abstract: In the context of a spatially flat FLRW background, we perform a symmetry classification of $k$-essence models with Lagrangian densities of the form $f_1(φ)R+f_2(φ,X)$. The kinetic term $X$ is introduced as an independent degree of freedom via a Lagrange multiplier, and the lapse function is treated as a dynamical variable. The symmetry analysis is applied to the constrained system prior to any gauge fixing. This approach reveals symmetries which otherwise are lost when the lapse is fixed at the level of the action. The derived families of $k$-essence models admitting nontrivial symmetries fall to two general classes: minimally coupled and nonminimally coupled theories to gravity. We use the corresponding Noetherian conservation laws to derive exact cosmological solutions. We find that, when the numerical value of the conserved charges is zero, the field equations reduce to an algebraic relation. We subsequently derive power-law expressions for the scale factor with exponents determined by the particular $k$-essence function.

</details>


### [107] [Testing charged bumblebee black holes through high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations in X-ray binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39864)
*Qi-Qi Liang,De-Jiang Yin,Zi-Qiang Cai,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有自发洛伦兹破缺效应的静态球对称带电bumblebee黑洞中的强场轨道动力学，通过分析类光与类时圆轨道得到光球半径、最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）及高频准周期振荡（HFQPO）的特征频率；引入有效参数集Θ=(M,X,C,β)刻画洛伦兹破缺与电荷的复合效应，并利用三颗黑洞X射线双星的HFQPO观测数据进行贝叶斯MCMC参数推断；结果显示复合参数C在68%置信区间内均兼容RN极限C=1，未发现统计显著的洛伦兹破缺修正，且HFQPO本身无法区分洛伦兹破缺与电荷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验自发洛伦兹对称性破缺（LSB）在强引力场中的可观测效应，特别是通过黑洞周围高频准周期振荡（HFQPOs）这一高精度天体物理探针约束bumblebee理论中的基本参数（l₁, l₂, Q₀/M）。

Method: 基于静态球对称带电bumblebee黑洞度规，求解测地线方程，导出光球半径r_ph、ISCO半径r_ISCO及相对论进动模型下的HFQPO三频（νₙ, νₜ, νₚ）；构建含M、X=r/M、l₁、l₂、Q₀/M的频率表达式，识别其组合简并性，定义有效参数C与β；采用贝叶斯MCMC方法对GRO J1655–40、XTE J1550–564和GRS 1915+105的HFQPO数据进行联合拟合。

Result: 所有三颗源的复合参数C的68%可信区间均包含Reissner–Nordström极限C=1；HFQPO频率对(l₁,l₂,Q₀/M)存在本质简并：不同参数组给出完全相同的QPO预测；未获得对单个LSB参数l₁、l₂或电荷Q₀/M的独立约束。

Conclusion: 在当前HFQPO观测精度下，无法证实bumblebee黑洞模型中存在统计显著的自发洛伦兹破缺效应；HFQPO作为单一探针不足以分离洛伦兹破缺与电磁荷的贡献；需结合其他独立观测（如引力波、事件视界成像、铁线谱等）以打破参数简并并提升对基础物理参数的限制能力。

Abstract: We investigate strong-field orbital dynamics for static spherically symmetric charged bumblebee black holes featuring spontaneous Lorentz-violating effects. Analysis of null and timelike circular geodesics yields the photon-sphere radius, the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) radius, and characteristic orbital frequencies for test particles. Within the relativistic precession model for high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (HFQPOs), observed twin-peak frequencies are governed by the full set of fundamental parameters $M$, $X=r/M$, $l_1$, $l_2$, and $Q_0/M$. Among them, $l_1$, $l_2$, and $Q_0/M$ enter frequency expressions only in combined forms, giving rise to intrinsic parameter degeneracy. An effective-parameter set $Θ=(M,X,C,β)$ is accordingly introduced to characterize these composite contributions. Bayesian MCMC parameter inference is carried out using HFQPO observational data from three black-hole X-ray binaries: GRO J1655--40, XTE J1550--564, and GRS 1915+105. The 68\% credible intervals of composite parameter $C$ all contain the Reissner--Nordström limit $C=1$, revealing no statistically significant net Lorentz-violating correction under our model assumptions. Distinct triples $(l_1,l_2,Q_0/M)$ yield identical QPO predictions, so HFQPO observations alone cannot disentangle Lorentz-violating and charge-related effects; precise constraints on these fundamental quantities require additional, more precise observational data.

</details>


### [108] [Zero modes and oscillatory instabilities of a Lorentz-violating Kalb-Ramond field on a Schwarzschild background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39936)
*Hong-Da Lyu,Zhi Xiao,Zhong-Xi Yu,Shoulong Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定Schwarzschild背景上、具有四次对称性破缺势与非最小Riemann耦合的Lorentz破缺Kalb–Ramond场的平衡构型与线性扰动，发现单极电响应在临界参数处呈现平方根发散，而高阶多极存在精确零频模；偶极模式在强负Riemann耦合下发生虚频模合并，并产生实部非零的振荡不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lorentz破缺背景下张量场（Kalb–Ramond场）在黑洞时空中的稳定性和动力学行为，特别是非最小耦合与对称性破缺势对静态解与线性谱的影响。

Method: 在固定Schwarzschild度规下求解Kalb–Ramond场的EOM；分析静态球对称解的代数约束结构；对线性扰动分离变量，推导径向方程并解析求解零频模；对ℓ=1扰动进行数值/渐近分析，识别虚频模合并与复频分支的临界行为。

Result: 1) 单极电分量由特征函数代数决定，其双根对应临界点，电响应呈(γ_c−γ)^(−1/2)发散；2) 所有ℓ≥0存在精确零频模ξ_{ℓn}=−(ℓ+n+1)(ℓ+n+2)/3；3) ℓ=1时出现两支低频虚频模，当Riemann耦合α→α_c^−时，ω_i±合并后分裂为共轭复频ω_±=±ω_R+iω_I，且ω_R∝√(α_c−α)。

Conclusion: 该模型中Lorentz破缺与非最小耦合共同诱导出新颖的代数增强效应与振荡不稳定性，区别于传统动力学不稳定性机制；零频模塔揭示了潜在的规范/拓扑结构，而复频分裂表明系统经历Andronov–Hopf型临界相变。

Abstract: We study equilibrium configurations and linear perturbations of a Lorentz-violating Kalb--Ramond field with a quartic symmetry-breaking potential and a nonminimal Riemann coupling on a fixed Schwarzschild background. For static spherical configurations, the electric component is determined algebraically by a characteristic function that can develop a finite-radius double root. Approaching this degenerate configuration, the monopole electric response scales as $|\widehat e(ω,r_c)|\propto(γ_c-γ)^{-1/2}$, while the propagating monopole amplitude remains regular, showing that the enhancement originates from the algebraic constraint rather than from a dynamical instability. For higher multipoles, we obtain an exact tower of zero-frequency modes, $ξ_{\ell n}=-(\ell+n+1)(\ell+n+2)/3$. For $\ell=1$, the finite-frequency spectrum contains two distinct low-frequency branches. As the Riemann coupling becomes more negative, the corresponding purely imaginary unstable modes coalesce and leave the imaginary axis as $ω_\pm=\pmω_R+iω_I$, producing an oscillatory instability. Near the merger, the real-part splitting follows a square-root law.

</details>


### [109] [On the topology of the space of vacuum initial data sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39987)
*Romain Gicquaud,Jonathan Glöckle*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了闭流形上真空初值数据集空间通常具有许多非平凡的同伦群，通过参数化共形方法将满足严格主能量条件的初值数据的非平凡同伦元推广到真空情形，并建立了York分解在度量子空间上的整体连续性与TT张量丛的平凡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中真空初值数据集空间的整体拓扑结构，特别是其同伦性质，此前尚无相关全局拓扑结果。

Method: 采用参数化的共形方法；关键工具包括：(1) 去除 admitting conformal Killing vectors 的度量后不改变度量空间的弱同伦型；(2) 在剩余度量上实现族参数化的York分解，且TT张量构成平凡Hilbert丛。

Result: 真空初值数据集空间π_k（k≥1）常含非平凡元素；TT张量在无CKV度量空间上形成平凡Hilbert丛；York分解可连续依赖于背景度量参数。

Conclusion: 真空初值数据集空间具有丰富且非平凡的同伦结构，这是该空间首个全局拓扑刻画结果。

Abstract: We show that the space of vacuum initial data sets on a closed manifold often has many non-trivial homotopy groups. The starting point is a result of the second named author, which constructs non-trivial elements in the homotopy groups of the space of initial data sets satisfying the strict dominant energy condition, together with a later result in joint work with Bernd Ammann showing that these elements often persist when the strictness assumption is dropped. In this work, we use a parametrized version of the conformal method to show that these elements may also be represented by maps into the space of vacuum initial data sets. This requires two results that may be of independent interest: metrics admitting conformal Killing vectors can be removed from the space of metrics without changing its weak homotopy type, and over the remaining metrics the York decomposition can be carried out in families, the TT-tensors forming a trivial Hilbert bundle. To our knowledge, this is the first result on the global topology of this space.

</details>


### [110] [Projective Symmetry and Its Breaking in Quadratic Metric-Affine Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39988)
*Carmen Ferrara,María José Guzmán,Laur Järv*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四维宇称偶的度规-仿射引力中广义投影对称性及其破缺，通过线性曲率与至多二次挠率和非度规性的作用量，系统分析了投影变换对仿射几何不可约分量的作用、不同投影不变理论的耦合结构、Noether恒等式、连接场方程的求解方法，以及与Dirac、电磁和复Klein-Gordon场的耦合机制；指出轴向-投影对称性对应手征旋转，矢量-投影对称性关联U(1)规范对称性，而精确引力投影不变性使部分连接方向成为非动力学模，其破缺则生成辅助场，从而统一刻画仿射规范模、约束物质耦合并关联引力对称性破缺与有效物质相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解度规-仿射引力中广义投影对称性的物理含义及其破缺机制，以识别仿射连接中的规范自由度、约束物质场耦合形式，并建立引力对称性破缺与低能有效物质相互作用之间的联系。

Method: 基于线性曲率、二次挠率与非度规性的最简度规-仿射作用量，推导广义投影变换在仿射几何不可约分解下的作用；分类轴向、度规迹及完全投影不变理论的耦合条件；利用Noether定理导出连接场方程的矢量型恒等式；发展对称适配的真空连接方程求解方法；系统分析与标准模型类物质场（Dirac、Maxwell、Klein-Gordon）的最小与非最小耦合；引入Stückelberg机制处理对称性破缺。

Result: 完全投影不变性将12个引力参数约化为5参数族，并产生4个矢量Noether恒等式；连接算符在投影不变下奇异，需对称适配方法求解；轴向-投影变换对应无质量费米子的手征旋转；矢量-投影变换由协矢量实现，等价于标量U(1)规范对称性；标准Maxwell理论与连接无关，但挠率依赖的Maxwell类推广可在引入补偿Stückelberg场后同时保持电磁与投影不变性；精确投影不变性使相应连接方向非动力学，其显式可控破缺将其转化为辅助场。

Conclusion: 广义投影对称性是度规-仿射引力中识别仿射规范模、限制物质耦合结构、并桥接引力对称性破缺与有效物质相互作用的核心原理；其破缺机制自然导出辅助场动力学，为构建与标准模型兼容的扩展引力理论提供对称性框架。

Abstract: We investigate generalized projective symmetry and its breaking in four-dimensional parity-even metric-affine gravity, considering an action linear in curvature and at most quadratic in torsion and nonmetricity. We derive the action of the generalized projective transformation on the irreducible components of the affine geometry and determine the coupling relations defining the axial, metric-trace, and fully projectively invariant theories. Complete invariance reduces the $12$ gravitational coefficients to a five-parameter family and generates four vectorial Noether identities for the connection equations. Projective invariance makes the connection operator singular, thus we develop a symmetry-adapted method for solving its vacuum field equations. We then analyze couplings to Dirac, electromagnetic, and complex Klein-Gordon fields. Locally exact axial-projective transformations correspond to chiral rotations for massless fermions at the classical level, while a combination of covectors acts as an Abelian connection linking a locally exact vector-projective representative to scalar $U(1)$ symmetry. Standard Maxwell theory is independent of the affine connection, whereas a torsion-dependent Maxwell-like extension can preserve both electromagnetic and projective invariance through compensating Stückelberg fields. Exact gravitational projective invariance makes the corresponding connection directions nondynamical and forces matter currents sourcing them to vanish. Controlled explicit breaking lifts these zero modes and converts them into auxiliary fields. Thus, projective symmetry provides a unified principle for identifying affine gauge modes, constraining matter couplings, and relating symmetry breaking in the gravitational sector to effective matter interactions.

</details>


### [111] [Axion corrections to photon superradiant scattering by Kerr black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40166)
*Zhi-Qing Zhu,Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子-光子耦合对克尔黑洞光子超辐射散射的影响，通过求解相对论性耦合方程，区分辐射区与亚阈值区，并发现轴子质量与频率关系决定光子放大是增强还是抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轴子暗物质候选者如何影响黑洞电磁超辐射过程，进而探测新物理及约束轴子参数空间。

Method: 在完全相对论框架下求解轴子场与电磁场的耦合波动方程；依据轴子频率与质量关系划分辐射区（ω > ma）与亚阈值区（ω < ma），分析能量输运与准束缚态激发。

Result: 在辐射区，轴子可传播至无穷远并带走能量，抑制光子超辐射放大；在亚阈值区，轴子被质量势垒局域化，通过准束缚态与黑洞视界交换能量，导致光子放大可被增强或抑制，取决于相关轴子模式是否满足超辐射条件（ω < mΩH）。

Conclusion: 轴子-光子耦合显著调制克尔黑洞的电磁超辐射行为，其效应具有频率-质量依赖性，为利用黑洞作为高精度轴子探测器提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study photon superradiant scattering by a Kerr black hole in the presence of an axion-photon coupling and investigate how this coupling modifies the electromagnetic amplification factor. We solve the coupled axion and electromagnetic equations in a fully relativistic framework. We distinguish two regimes according to the relation between the sourced axion frequency and the axion mass. In the radiative regime, the axion can propagate to spatial infinity and carry away energy, thereby suppressing photon amplification. In the subthreshold regime, the axion is confined by the mass barrier and exchanges energy with the black-hole horizon through quasibound-state excitations. In this regime, photon amplification can be either enhanced or suppressed, depending on whether the relevant axion modes satisfy the superradiance condition.

</details>


### [112] [Flat limit of artificial cosmology for scalar waves in Schwarzschild-de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40243)
*Anıl Zenginoğlu,Govind Arun Kumar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究小宇宙学常数下宇宙学波形如何逼近孤立系统的波形，通过Schwarzschild–de Sitter背景上的标量波进行定量分析，发现适当选取的平坦极限能良好恢复渐近平直波形的主要特征，但人工宇宙学方法虽改善铃荡阶段匹配，却无法准确再现中间多项式衰减尾部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到的宇宙学常数极小值，以及Misner提出的利用共形紧化引入的发散项需通过宇宙学常数进行正则化的思想。

Method: 在Schwarzschild–de Sitter时空上求解标量波方程；构造保持未来共形边界正则性的foliation；比较宇宙学与渐近平直波形在几何匹配初值下的行为；引入不改变近区几何的宇宙学源以推广Misner人工宇宙学模型。

Result: 平坦极限数值稳定，能恢复渐近平直波形的核心特征（如铃荡）；改进的人工宇宙学模型提升了铃荡阶段波形一致性，但未能准确复现中间区域的多项式衰减（polynomial tail）行为。

Conclusion: 小Λ宇宙学波形在适当foliation下可作为渐近平直波形的有效近似，但其尾部动力学对宇宙学结构敏感，不能仅由近区几何决定；Misner正则化思想具有启发性，但需更精细的源构造或边界处理以完整刻画迟滞尾部。

Abstract: How do cosmological waveforms approximate waveforms from isolated systems for a small cosmological constant? This question is motivated by the observed small value of the cosmological constant, and also by Misner's idea to regulate the formally singular terms introduced into the Einstein equations by conformal compactification. We investigate this question quantitatively using scalar waves on Schwarzschild--de Sitter backgrounds. A suitable flat limit requires the foliation to remain regular at the future conformal boundary. We compare bridge foliations extending from the black hole horizon to the cosmological horizon with a regular asymptotically flat limit. Comparisons of cosmological and asymptotically flat waveforms on geometrically matched data show that the flat limit is numerically well behaved and recovers the essential features of the asymptotically flat waveform. We extend Misner's artificial cosmology by introducing a cosmological source that leaves the near-zone geometry unchanged and show that it improves agreement of the ringdown waveform but fails to capture the intermediate polynomial tail behavior accurately.

</details>


### [113] [Slowly rotating Black Holes in DHOST Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17614)
*Hugo Candan,Karim Noui,David Langlois*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了退化高阶标量-张量（DHOST）理论中缓慢旋转黑洞解，利用Hartle-Thorne近似推导出仅依赖径向坐标的拖曳函数ω的可积微分方程，并证明角向依赖被视界与无穷远正则性条件排除；进而以具主干发（primary hair）的黑洞为例，分析旋转对赤道面内最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）和光子环的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展广义相对论外的引力理论中黑洞物理的可观测特征，特别是DHOST理论中旋转效应是否引入新自由度或破坏正则性。

Method: 基于静态球对称DHOST黑洞背景，采用Hartle-Thorne弱场慢转展开；严格求解轴对称扰动中frame-dragging函数ω的径向方程，并通过边界正则性分析排除θ-依赖项。

Result: 证明任意允许静态解的DHOST理论中ω(r)方程完全可积，得到显式解；证实ω不能含θ依赖，与GR一致；旋转显著调制ISCO半径及赤道光子轨道，其偏移量携带DHOST耦合参数信息。

Conclusion: DHOST理论中缓慢旋转黑洞的轴对称结构与GR保持相同自由度（仅ω(r)），但可观测轨道动力学（如ISCO、光子环）对高阶标量耦合敏感，为未来引力波与EHT观测提供理论判据。

Abstract: We study slowly rotating black hole solutions within Degenerate Higher Order Scalar Tensor (DHOST) theories. Starting from a static, spherically symmetric metric solution of a DHOST theory, we employ the Hartle-Thorne ansatz to model a slowly rotating spacetime. We show that the differential equation governing the frame-dragging function $ω$ (which is supposed to depend on the radial coordinate only) is integrable for any DHOST theory allowing us to obtain its explicit form. We also consider angular dependence in $ω$ and show that regularity at the horizon and at infinity forbids it, as in General Relativity. As an illustration of the formalism introduced here, we study the slowly-rotating version of black hole solutions with primary hair obtained recently, examining the influence of the rotation on the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) and on the circular light trajectories in the equatorial plane.

</details>
